province_event = {
	id = 148001
	title = "0"
	desc = "0"
	trigger = {
		is_core = ZUL
		NOT = { culture = zulu }
		ZUL = { exists = no }
		OR = {
			any_neighbor_province = {
				NOT = { is_core = ZUL }
			}
			NOT = {
				any_neighbor_province = {
					culture = zulu
				}
			}
		}
		owner = { AI = yes }
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1
	}
	immediate = {
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = ZUL
			}
			set_province_flag = ZULcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = ZUL
				NOT = { has_province_flag = ZULcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = ZULcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = ZUL
				NOT = { has_province_flag = ZULcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = ZULthree_cores
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = ZULcounted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		remove_core = ZUL
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				any_neighbor_province = {
					has_province_flag = ZULthree_cores
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						is_core = ZUL
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						culture = zulu
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "2"
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = ZULthree_cores
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						has_province_flag = ZULthree_cores
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

province_event = {
	id = 148011
	title = "0"
	desc = "0"
	trigger = {
		is_core = TUN
		NOT = { culture = maghrebi }
		TUN = { exists = no }
		OR = {
			any_neighbor_province = {
				NOT = { is_core = TUN }
			}
			NOT = {
				any_neighbor_province = {
					culture = maghrebi
				}
			}
		}
		owner = { AI = yes }
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1
	}
	immediate = {
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = TUN
			}
			set_province_flag = TUNcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = TUN
				NOT = { has_province_flag = TUNcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = TUNcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = TUN
				NOT = { has_province_flag = TUNcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = TUNthree_cores
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = TUNcounted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		remove_core = TUN
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				any_neighbor_province = {
					has_province_flag = TUNthree_cores
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						is_core = TUN
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						culture = maghrebi
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "2"
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = TUNthree_cores
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						has_province_flag = TUNthree_cores
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		is_core = SRI
		NOT = { culture = sinhala }
		SRI = { exists = no }
		OR = {
			any_neighbor_province = {
				NOT = { is_core = SRI }
			}
			NOT = {
				any_neighbor_province = {
					culture = sinhala
				}
			}
		}
		owner = { AI = yes }
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1
	}
	immediate = {
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = SRI
			}
			set_province_flag = SRIcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = SRI
				NOT = { has_province_flag = SRIcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = SRIcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = SRI
				NOT = { has_province_flag = SRIcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = SRIthree_cores
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = SRIcounted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		remove_core = SRI
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				any_neighbor_province = {
					has_province_flag = SRIthree_cores
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						is_core = SRI
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						culture = sinhala
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "2"
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = SRIthree_cores
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						has_province_flag = SRIthree_cores
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		is_core = EGY
		NOT = { culture = misri }
		EGY = { exists = no }
		OR = {
			any_neighbor_province = {
				NOT = { is_core = EGY }
			}
			NOT = {
				any_neighbor_province = {
					culture = misri
				}
			}
		}
		owner = { AI = yes }
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1
	}
	immediate = {
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = EGY
			}
			set_province_flag = EGYcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = EGY
				NOT = { has_province_flag = EGYcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = EGYcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = EGY
				NOT = { has_province_flag = EGYcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = EGYthree_cores
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = EGYcounted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		remove_core = EGY
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				any_neighbor_province = {
					has_province_flag = EGYthree_cores
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						is_core = EGY
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						culture = misri
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "2"
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = EGYthree_cores
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						has_province_flag = EGYthree_cores
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		is_core = FRM
		NOT = { culture = north_german }
		FRM = { exists = no }
		OR = {
			any_neighbor_province = {
				NOT = { is_core = FRM }
			}
			NOT = {
				any_neighbor_province = {
					culture = north_german
				}
			}
		}
		owner = { AI = yes }
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1
	}
	immediate = {
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = FRM
			}
			set_province_flag = FRMcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = FRM
				NOT = { has_province_flag = FRMcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = FRMcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = FRM
				NOT = { has_province_flag = FRMcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = FRMthree_cores
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = FRMcounted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		remove_core = FRM
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				any_neighbor_province = {
					has_province_flag = FRMthree_cores
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						is_core = FRM
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						culture = north_german
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "2"
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = FRMthree_cores
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						has_province_flag = FRMthree_cores
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		is_core = RUS
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				culture = ugrian
				culture = byelorussian
				culture = ukrainian
				culture = russian
			}
		}
		RUS = { exists = no }
		OR = {
			any_neighbor_province = {
				NOT = { is_core = RUS }
			}
			NOT = {
				any_neighbor_province = {
					OR = {
						culture = ugrian
						culture = byelorussian
						culture = ukrainian
						culture = russian
					}
				}
			}
		}
		owner = { AI = yes }
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1
	}
	immediate = {
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = RUS
			}
			set_province_flag = RUScounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = RUS
				NOT = { has_province_flag = RUScounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = RUScounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = RUS
				NOT = { has_province_flag = RUScounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = RUSthree_cores
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = RUScounted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		remove_core = RUS
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				any_neighbor_province = {
					has_province_flag = RUSthree_cores
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						is_core = RUS
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						OR = {
							culture = ugrian
							culture = byelorussian
							culture = ukrainian
							culture = russian
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "2"
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = RUSthree_cores
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						has_province_flag = RUSthree_cores
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1
	}
	immediate = {
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = BAL
			}
			set_province_flag = BALcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = BAL
				NOT = { has_province_flag = BALcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = BALcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = BAL
				NOT = { has_province_flag = BALcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = BALthree_cores
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = BALcounted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		remove_core = BAL
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				any_neighbor_province = {
					has_province_flag = BALthree_cores
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						is_core = BAL
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {

						culture = malay
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	}
	immediate = {
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = PNM
			}
			set_province_flag = PNMcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = PNM
				NOT = { has_province_flag = PNMcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = PNMcounted
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				is_core = PNM
				NOT = { has_province_flag = PNMcounted }
			}
			set_province_flag = PNMthree_cores
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = PNMcounted
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "1"
		remove_core = PNM
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				any_neighbor_province = {
					has_province_flag = PNMthree_cores
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						is_core = PNM
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						culture = central_american
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "2"
		any_neighbor_province = {
			clr_province_flag = PNMthree_cores
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				NOT = {
					any_neighbor_province = {
						has_province_flag = PNMthree_cores
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
